### [1] [Verify, Don't Trust: Agentic Model Development for Video Discovery Retrieval at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21257)
*Hao Fu,Baiting Zhu,Minglei Chen,Yinjie Huang,Shuai Ding*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoPilot，一种人类把关的长周期在线自动研究方法，通过角色化智能体、版本化领域技能、类型化适配器和持久化记录，避免无效对比与结论误判，并在37天VDD检索系统研究中验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动研究循环在分钟级自包含程序上可行，但在线自动研究涉及异步系统、数小时变体和数周活动，完成运行仍可能因空操作、数据窗口泄漏、评估语义漂移或服务漏斗差异而产生无效结论。

Method: 提出EvoPilot：人类把关的长期在线自动研究方法。角色特定智能体通过版本化领域技能和类型化适配器执行每轮；持久记录保存实验与失败；确定性检查强制已记录经验。用于支持Video Deep Dive的检索系统，37天、七个方向、每小时刷新数亿视频索引。

Result: 早期人工实验未确立交互头收益；原始自动研究误将离线命中率下降22个百分点归因于该头。EvoPilot人类把关验证发现该下降源于既有评估缺陷（输出深度3000和600），修复后匹配对比测得离线提升3.20个百分点。回放与变异测试拒绝无效比较、接纳有效对照；恢复中断轮次并复用工件节省约5 GPU小时。7天随机在线评估显示VDD切片GSRR相对提升0.66%。

Conclusion: EvoPilot可在长周期在线自动研究中保障实验有效性与结论可靠性，兼顾错误拦截、状态恢复和资源复用，并在真实检索系统中获得在线收益。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can propose, implement, and evaluate model changes. Autoresearch loops demonstrate this capability through minutes-scale iterations on a self-contained program. Online autoresearch instead spans asynchronous systems, hours-long variants, and weeks-long campaigns that can influence a product. A completed run can still support an invalid conclusion when a code change is a no-op, data windows leak, evaluator semantics drift, or the two arms traverse different serving funnels. We present EvoPilot, a human-gated method for long-horizon online autoresearch. Role-specific agents execute each round through a versioned domain skill and typed adapter. Durable records preserve experiments and failures; deterministic checks enforce recorded lessons.
  We study a 37-day campaign for the retrieval system that powers Video Deep Dive (VDD), an online experience for discovering follow-on videos after a user opens a seed video. The campaign covered seven directions and used an hourly refreshed index of hundreds of millions of videos. Earlier manual experiments had not established a benefit from an interaction head. A primitive autoresearch attempt revisited the direction but incorrectly attributed an offline hit-rate decline of 22 percentage points to the head. We then introduced EvoPilot. Its human-gated verification traced the drop to a pre-existing evaluation defect that produced output depths of 3,000 and 600. After repair, a matched comparison measured an offline improvement of 3.20 percentage points. Post-study replay and mutation tests rejected invalid comparisons while admitting valid counterparts. Durable state recovered an interrupted round, and artifact reuse avoided approximately five GPU-hours. Separately, a seven-day randomized online evaluation estimated a 0.66% relative increase in the VDD slice of Good Search Result Rate for Retention (GSRR).

</details>


### [2] [Hybrid GPU-CPU Retrieval for Personalized Search at Ultra-Large Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21281)
*Hao Fu,Jichao Sun,Baiting Zhu,Qiaoling Liu,Yan Shi,Cheng Lu,Liu Liu,Yubo Wang,Xin Yao,Xiangyu Niu,Xu Dong,Wenhan Lyu,Chiyao Shen,Yinjie Huang,Minglei Chen,Shuai Ding,Li Fan,Xiao Kong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 GPU-CPU 混合协同服务系统，通过编排而非新模型解决"个性化-规模悖论"：GPU 路径负责十亿级文档的深度个性化检索与交互预排序，CPU 路径负责约二十倍规模库存的广度轻量检索，两路可单独或同时运行，候选去重后共享下游排序。生产环境 A/B 测试验证了相关性与参与度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 万亿级用户生成内容的嵌入检索面临"个性化-规模悖论"：将全部服务库存放入 GPU 显存过于耗费资源，而 CPU 算力又无法在延迟关键路径上运行同样交互密集的模型。需要同时满足深度个性化与大规模库存覆盖，并在固定延迟与资源预算下运行。

Method: 采用混合 GPU-CPU 协同服务架构，以编排而非新模型类别解决矛盾。深度 GPU 路径在约十亿文档的精选在线池上融合检索与交互预排序；广度 CPU 路径在约二十倍规模的独立精选在线库存上执行轻量个性化打分。每次请求可运行其中一条或两条路径，候选在进入共享下游排序前先去重。并通过检索日志、生产延迟测量与匹配容量规划进行分析。

Result: 系统已在生产环境部署。全系统 A/B 测试相对传统纯 CPU 配置提升了模型打分的相关性与实质性参与度；单独路径实验也显示各自部署范围内的正向价值。检索日志表明两条路径贡献结构上不同的候选，生产服务测量刻画了其延迟表现，匹配容量规划量化了将建模深度分配给 GPU、库存广度分配给 CPU 的经济合理性。

Conclusion: 结果验证了一种实用、可独立演进的"深度-广度"架构，适用于超大规模个性化搜索，证明通过编排现有资源即可调和个性化与规模的冲突。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval on user-generated content at the trillion-document scale exposes a sharp conflict between two production demands: deep, expressive personalization for queries with rich user intent, and broad coverage of a massive inventory under fixed latency and resource budgets. We characterize this as the personalization-scale paradox: hosting the full serving inventory in GPU memory is too resource intensive, while CPU compute cannot execute the same interaction-heavy model on the latency-critical path.
  We present a hybrid GPU-CPU co-serving system that resolves the paradox through orchestration rather than a new model class. A high-depth GPU pathway fuses retrieval and interaction pre-ranking over a curated online pool on the order of a billion documents, while a high-breadth CPU pathway searches an independently selected online inventory roughly twenty times larger with lightweight personalized scoring. Either or both pathways can run per request; candidates are deduplicated before shared downstream ranking.
  The system is deployed in production. A full-system A/B test against the legacy CPU-only configuration improves model-scored relevance and substantive engagement, while separate pathway experiments show positive value at their own deployment scopes. Retrieval logs show that the pathways contribute structurally distinct candidates, production serving measurements characterize their latency, and a matched capacity plan quantifies the economic rationale for assigning modeling depth to GPUs and inventory breadth to CPUs. Together, these results validate a practical, independently evolvable depth-breadth architecture for ultra-large-scale personalized search.

</details>


### [3] [Auto-Bidding with Disentangled Advertiser Profiles and Train-Free Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21308)
*Songyue Cai,Shan Gu,Wei Chen,Ziru Xu,Lianyu Wang,Jian Xu,Xiaofeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出无训练自适应的解耦广告主画像自动出价框架ADAPT


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于画像的方法在推荐等领域有效，但在自动出价中应用有限：纯画像提取困难、公共与私有信息难以同时建模、画像更新与冷启动适应存在挑战。

Method: 两阶段训练范式加无训练自适应。(i)阶段一通过对比学习在广告主记忆库上提取纯静态与动态画像；(ii)阶段二将动态画像解耦为公共画像与私有画像，与静态画像共同条件化出价策略；(iii)训练完成后，可在不重训的情况下为新广告主构建画像并为已有广告主更新画像。

Result: 在大规模自动出价基准上持续取得更优性能，消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: ADAPT通过解耦广告主画像与无训练自适应有效解决了画像构建与更新难题，代码将开源。

Abstract: Auto-bidding is a key component of modern advertising systems that provides a personalized bidding strategy for each advertiser. By characterizing each individual, profile-based methods achieve personalization and have proven effective in domains such as recommendation. However, despite the diverse bidding behavior of advertisers, their application to auto-bidding remains limited. A primary reason is that constructing and leveraging advertiser profiles face several challenges: extracting pure profiles is non-trivial, modeling common and private information simultaneously is difficult, and profile updating and cold-start adaptation remain challenging. To tackle these issues, we propose \textbf{ADAPT}, an \underline{\textbf{A}}uto-bidding framework with \underline{\textbf{D}}isentangled \underline{\textbf{A}}dvertiser \underline{\textbf{P}}rofiles and \underline{\textbf{T}}raining-free adaptation. ADAPT introduces a two-stage training paradigm and supports training-free adaptation. Specifically, (i) the stage 1 extracts pure static and dynamic profiles via contrastive learning over the advertiser memory bank; (ii) the stage 2 disentangles the dynamic profile into a common profile and a private profile, and combines them with the static profile to jointly condition the bidding strategy; (iii) once trained, ADAPT constructs profiles for new advertisers and updates profiles of existing advertisers without retraining. Our experiments on a large-scale auto-bidding benchmark demonstrate that ADAPT consistently achieves superior performance, and ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of each module. The source code will be released at https://github.com/YuzunoKawori/ADAPT.

</details>


### [4] [Adaptive Preference Modeling via Explicit Indirect Relational Learning for Personalized Fashion Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21475)
*Shuiying Liao,Li Li,P. Y. Mok*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出APCL框架，通过显式建模直接与间接关系并结合对比学习，提升稀疏多模态数据下的个性化时尚互补推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化时尚互补推荐需要在稀疏且多模态的数据条件下同时建模用户偏好和物品兼容性。现有方法多通过图传播隐式捕捉高阶关系信号或依赖直接交互数据，无法显式建模间接的偏好与兼容性关系，从而限制了推荐效果。

Method: APCL构建统一推荐架构，显式建模直接与间接关系信号。通过相关性引导的自适应聚合机制生成间接的用户-物品与物品-物品关系，并表示为专门的个性化视图和兼容性视图；引入功能性视图对比学习策略，对齐直接与间接的偏好表示以及直接与间接的兼容性表示，促使跨关系上下文的一致性；同时融合多模态视觉与文本信息。

Result: 在两个基准时尚推荐数据集上的实验表明，APCL持续优于代表性基线方法，并在稀疏交互场景下表现出更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显式建模间接关系并结合对比学习与多模态信息，能够更丰富地捕捉语义特征，有效提升稀疏交互条件下的时尚互补推荐性能。

Abstract: Personalized fashion complementary recommendation requires jointly modeling user preferences and item compatibility under sparse and multimodal data conditions. Existing approaches often capture higher-order relational signals implicitly through graph propagation or rely on direct interaction data, limiting their ability to explicitly model indirect preference and compatibility relationships. To address this limitation, we propose an Adaptive Preference with Contrastive Learning framework (APCL) that explicitly models both direct and indirect relational signals within a unified recommendation architecture. Specifically, APCL constructs indirect user-item and item-item relationships through a correlation-guided adaptive aggregation mechanism and represents them as dedicated personalization and compatibility views. To improve representation learning, we further introduce a functional view contrastive learning strategy that aligns direct and indirect preference representations and direct and indirect compatibility representations, encouraging consistency across relational contexts. By integrating multimodal visual and textual information with explicit indirect relational modeling, APCL captures richer semantic characteristics while improving robustness in sparse-interaction settings. Experiments on two benchmark fashion recommendation datasets demonstrate that APCL consistently outperforms representative baseline methods.

</details>


### [5] [Predictable Failure in Multi-Hop Retrieval: Score-Distributional Confidence Scoring and Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22056)
*Andre Bacellar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文证明多跳检索失败在结构可预测的子群体中聚集（CWAR可归约性、特征机制互补性），并提出无需额外LLM调用的RegimeAbstain方法，基于检索置信度分数(RCS)实现校准弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳检索失败并非在查询间均匀分布，而是聚集于结构上可预测的子群体中。作者希望形式化这一结构，并利用它来降低"自信错误答案"（置信但错误）的比例，从而在有限覆盖率下提升可靠性。

Method: 提出两条理论结果：(1) CWAR可归约性——自信失败的可归约性当且仅当检索特征携带关于成功的互信息，此条件在LLM-judge流程中满足而在dense-only中较弱，解释了两种机制的AUC-AC差距；(2) 特征机制互补性——没有单一ANN分数特征在所有失败机制中预测最优，主导特征因数据集而异，并给出构造性见证对。在此基础上实例化RegimeAbstain：计算最多九个查询-ANN结构特征的logistic函数RCS，无需额外LLM调用，用于校准弃权策略。定义CWAR指标，在三大多跳基准与两种检索架构、五种失败机制下评估。

Result: 在五种机制下，RCS对八个置信度基线的AUC-AC均为最优或并列最优。在MuSiQue(LLM-judge)上，50%覆盖率下CWAR从39.5%降至20.6%（相对降低47.8%），ECE=0.035。MuSiQue训练的模型迁移到2WikiMultiHopQA仅损失-0.5pp AUC，验证了机制特征的领域无关性。

Conclusion: 多跳检索失败具有可形式化的机制结构，可据此设计无需额外LLM调用的置信度与弃权机制，显著降低自信错误率并具备跨域迁移能力。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval failures are not uniformly distributed across queries: they cluster in structurally predictable subpopulations. We prove two results formalizing this structure. First (CWAR Reducibility): confident-failure reduction is achievable if and only if retrieval features carry mutual information about success, a condition satisfied by LLM-judge pipelines but substantially weaker in dense-only settings, explaining the AUC-AC gap between regimes. Second (Feature Regime Complementarity): no single ANN score feature achieves best predictive performance across all failure regimes; the dominant feature differs between datasets (query length on MuSiQue, hop-1 concentration on HoVer), and a constructive witness pair shows each is necessary in one regime and non-contributory in the other. We instantiate these principles in RegimeAbstain, which computes a Retrieval Confidence Score (RCS), a logistic function of up to nine query-ANN structural features, all available without any additional LLM call, and uses it to implement a calibrated abstention policy. We define the Confident-Wrong-Answer Rate (CWAR) metric and evaluate across three multi-hop benchmarks (MuSiQue, 2WikiMultiHopQA, HoVer) and two retrieval architectures (LLM-judge and dense-only), covering five failure regimes with CWAR from 14.5% to 62.1%. RCS achieves best or co-best AUC-AC in all five conditions against eight confidence baselines. On MuSiQue (LLM-judge), RCS reduces CWAR from 39.5% to 20.6% at 50% coverage (47.8% relative reduction), with ECE=0.035. A model trained on MuSiQue transfers to 2WikiMultiHopQA with only -0.5pp AUC loss, confirming the domain-agnostic structure of regime features.

</details>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [6] [How Much of a Real Workload Can LLM-Generated GPU Kernels Actually Reach?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21058)
*Gaurav Agarwal,Ashish Garg,Isha Singhal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文评估语言模型生成GPU内核的能力，并首次分析这些内核在真实模型端到端运行时间中的占比，同时揭示KernelBench正确性检查的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注语言模型能否写出优于PyTorch的GPU内核，但未回答这些内核在真实模型端到端运行时间中占多大比例。此外，KernelBench的评测方法可能存在漏洞，需要检验。

Method: 在KernelBench level 1上评测五种模型配置；对三个领域的七个工作负载进行性能剖析以测量可加速比例；提出DLRM-Bench（12个推荐系统内核问题）；用零张量测试KernelBench正确性检查并提出尺度不变的替代方案。

Result: 前沿模型在91.1%的问题上生成正确内核，56个中22个有独立验证的加速，中位数1.235x（含三个卷积）；开源模型最好仅30.4%正确、三个验证加速、零卷积。可加速比例在8.9%-58.2%之间；Transformer上80-86%时间用于cuBLAS GEMM和FlashAttention，端到端提升上限约1%且随模型规模缩小；推荐系统为58.2%。DLRM-Bench胜率41.7%、中位数1.552x，预计端到端8.63%。KernelBench的allclose检查在60个问题中有4个可被零张量满足，两个内核曾利用此漏洞，其中一个283x仅写入0.3%输出。

Conclusion: 语言模型生成GPU内核的能力虽有提升，但在真实工作负载中的端到端收益有限，尤其在Transformer上；KernelBench的正确性检查存在严重缺陷，需采用尺度不变的替代方案，作者发布全部879次评估。

Abstract: Language models can now write GPU kernels that outperform PyTorch. We evaluate five model configurations on KernelBench level 1 and find that a frontier model produces correct kernels for 91.1% of problems and independently verified speedups on 22 of 56, including three convolutions, with a median of 1.235x. Open-weights models are far behind: the best reaches 30.4% correct with three verified speedups and solves zero convolutions.
  We then ask a question the literature does not: what fraction of a real model's wall clock do such kernels govern? Profiling seven workloads across three domains, we find the addressable fraction ranges from 8.9% to 58.2%. On transformers, 80-86% of runtime is spent in cuBLAS GEMM and FlashAttention, bounding realistic end-to-end improvement at roughly 1%, and the fraction shrinks with model scale. On recommenders it is 58.2%, concentrated in a single embedding kernel. We introduce DLRM-Bench, 12 recommender kernel problems in KernelBench format, and measure a 41.7% win rate at a 1.552x median there, projecting 8.63% end-to-end.
  Separately, we show that KernelBench's correctness check (torch.allclose with an absolute tolerance) is satisfied by a tensor of zeros on 4 of 60 level-1 problems. Two kernels in our own results exploited this before we detected them, including one scored at 283x that wrote 0.3% of its output buffer. We propose scale-invariant replacements and release all 879 evaluations.

</details>


### [7] [DLB: Distributed Load Balancing at Scale for Generative AI Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21079)
*Santiago R. Balseiro,Bartek Wydrowski,Sameer Agarwal,David Applegate,Aaron Archer,Soheil Hassas Yeganeh,Alex Iriza,Bobby Kleinberg,Balasubramanian Sivan,Pranav Vaish,Oscar Zegarra,Wenxin Zhang,Vahab Mirrokni,Amin Vahdat*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 DLB（分布式负载均衡器），通过分布式对等探测与持续学习延迟模型，为大规模异构工作负载（如生成式 AI）最小化端到端用户延迟，并给出稳定性与全局性能的理论分析、仿真评估及 Google 22 个月生产部署经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据中心依赖稀缺昂贵的 GPU/TPU 等加速器，后端基础设施压力巨大。对于服务时间异构、多阶段处理的生成式 AI 类工作负载，传统负载均衡方法往往不足，只能依赖成本高昂的过度配置来维持服务水平目标。

Method: DLB 采用可扩展的分布式设计，利用对等探测（peer-to-peer probing）在大型地理分布式基础设施中实时掌握服务器容量；系统持续学习延迟模型以估计路由决策的延迟影响，从而有效管理异构硬件与多样模型架构；同时提供新的路由算法理论分析，证明其稳定性与随时间变化的全局性能保证。

Result: 大量仿真显示 DLB 相比最先进的负载均衡算法有显著收益。在 Google 为期 22 个月的生产部署中，DLB 支撑数千个机器学习模型、每秒数百万请求的大规模生成式 AI 推理；生产迁移分析表明其相对旧基线取得统计显著的延迟下降，中位延迟降低 17%、p95 尾部延迟降低 13%。

Conclusion: DLB 通过分布式对等探测与延迟模型学习，在理论与实践中均能有效降低大规模异构工作负载的端到端延迟，并在真实生产环境中验证了其可扩展性与显著性能提升，同时总结了生产系统的设计取舍与实践经验。

Abstract: The reliance on scarce and expensive accelerators such as GPUs and TPUs in modern datacenters places unprecedented demands on backend infrastructure. For workloads characterized by heterogeneous service times and complex multi-stage processing, such as Generative AI, conventional load balancing techniques are often inadequate, relying heavily on costly overprovisioning to maintain service level objectives. This paper introduces DLB, the Distributed Load Balancer, a novel system designed to minimize end-to-end user latency for large-scale, heterogeneous workloads.
  DLB employs a scalable, distributed design with peer-to-peer probing to maintain real-time visibility into server capacity across large-scale, geographically distributed infrastructure. The system continuously learns latency models to estimate the latency impact of routing decisions, allowing it to effectively manage heterogeneous hardware and diverse model architectures. We provide a novel theoretical analysis of our routing algorithms that establishes their stability and global performance guarantees over time. We also evaluate DLB through extensive simulations, which show substantial gains compared to state-of-the-art load balancing algorithms. Finally, following a 22-month deployment of DLB at Google, where it facilitates large-scale Generative AI inference for thousands of different machine learning models and millions of requests per second, we detail the design choices and practical experiences gained from the system in production. Analysis of production migrations demonstrates that DLB yields statistically significant latency reductions compared to the legacy baseline, including a 17\% decrease in median latency and a 13\% decrease at the p95 tail.

</details>


### [8] [SensorWF: A FAIR Generalizable Workflow Framework for Scientific Time-Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21110)
*Logan Luna,Joseph Rigo,Kellan Shew,Raul Alejandro Vargas-Acosta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 SensorWF——一个支持跨领域科学时间序列分析的 FAIR 标注工作流框架，通过五个可复用模块与领域适配器实现通用性，并在航天器遥测、动态心电图与大气气候三个领域验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学传感器数据广泛存在于航天工程、临床医学和大气科学等领域，但目前各领域的数据处理流程多为单体的、领域特定的脚本，存在隐式假设且跨领域复用性差，缺乏统一的通用分析框架。

Method: 提出 SensorWF 框架，包含带类型化输入/输出契约的五模块可复用核心（M1-M5）。采用领域适配器模式，将领域特定逻辑全部隔离在 M1 中，使 M2-M5 可跨学科一致运行；领域假设编码于机器可读的组件注册表中。框架生成带 SHA-256 校验和的运行时 PROV-O/ProvONE 溯源记录，并输出符合 SSN/SOSA 的 OWL 本体。

Result: 在航天器遥测、动态心电（ECG）和大气气候三个领域实例化框架，并演示合成故障注入与多检测器异常检测的用例扩展。结果表明，单一代码库仅通过每领域约 170-500 行的 M1 适配器参数化，即可支持采样率、通道数和故障分类各异的跨领域分析流程。所有代码、组件注册表、本体产物及数据集均作为开放科学对象公开。

Conclusion: SensorWF 通过适配器模式与 FAIR 溯源机制实现了科学时间序列分析的可泛化、可复用框架，显著降低了跨领域流程构建成本，代码公开于 https://purl.archive.org/sensor-wf。

Abstract: Scientific sensor data is foundational across disciplines including spacecraft engineering, clinical medicine, and atmospheric science. In each context, pipelines are constructed to ingest raw archives, assess data quality, perform feature engineering and semantic annotation, and record provenance. However, these pipelines are often implemented as monolithic, domain-specific scripts with implicit assumptions and limited reusability across fields. This work introduces SensorWF, a FAIR-annotated workflow framework for generalizable scientific time-series analysis. The framework features a five-module reusable core (M1-M5) with typed input/output contracts. A domain adapter pattern isolates all domain-specific logic within M1, enabling modules M2-M5 to operate identically across disciplines. Domain assumptions are encoded in a machine-readable component registry, enabling reuse across sensor domains without modifying the analytical core. SensorWF also generates runtime PROV-O/ProvONE provenance traces with SHA-256 checksums for all file-path entities and emits SSN/SOSA-aligned OWL ontologies as primary outputs. To assess generalizability, SensorWF is instantiated in three distinct scientific domains: spacecraft telemetry, ambulatory ECG, and atmospheric climate, with synthetic fault injection and multi-detector anomaly detection demonstrated as use-case extensions. Results show that a single codebase, parameterized solely through M1 adapters of approximately 170-500 lines each, supports analytical pipelines across domains with varying sampling rates, channel counts, and fault taxonomies. All code, the component registry, ontology artifacts, and datasets are made available as an open scientific object. Our codebase is publicly available at https://purl.archive.org/sensor-wf.

</details>


### [9] [Cloud-Side Transactional Orchestration Framework for Resource-Constrained Embedded Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21143)
*Pravin Nagare,Aditya Sabbineni,Preetam Dedu,Willison Lopes*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出面向资源受限嵌入式平台的T-BFF事务编排框架，将事务状态机与微服务编排迁移至云端，配合利用非易失闪存的"双握手"协议实现重启后状态恢复，在ARM Cortex-A53上实现最大堆占用降低35%、端到端事务延迟改善40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数字商务生态向低端消费电子延伸，硬件限制（有限的CPU占空比与易变的堆碎片）成为复杂交易流程的瓶颈。传统端侧中间件需要高"网络通信频度"来管理多步状态机，在不稳定的家庭网络上导致延迟上升与交易失败风险。因此需要一种在遗留硬件上仍能保证事务完整性的方案。

Method: 提出事务型Backend-for-Front-End（T-BFF）编排框架：把事务状态机与微服务编排迁移到云端层，从而大幅削减客户端开销；同时引入"双握手"协议，利用非易失闪存实现硬件重启后的状态恢复。实验在ARM Cortex-A53平台上进行验证。

Result: 在ARM Cortex-A53平台上，最大堆使用量降低35%，端到端事务延迟改善40%。

Conclusion: 该框架为在2026年IoT环境下、于遗留硬件上维持事务完整性提供了可扩展且可持续的蓝图。

Abstract: As digital commerce ecosystems expand into low-end consumer electronics (CE), hardware constraints-specifically limited CPU duty cycles and volatile heap fragmentation-become significant bottlenecks for complex transactional flows. Traditional on-device middleware requires high "network chattiness" to manage multi-step state machines, leading to increased latency and potential transaction failure on unstable residential networks. This paper proposes a novel Transactional Backend-for-Front-End (T-BFF) Orchestration Framework designed for resource-constrained embedded platforms. By migrating the transactional state machine and microservice orchestration to a cloud-side layer, we achieve a significant reduction in client-side overhead. Our framework introduces a "Double-Handshake" protocol utilizing non-volatile flash memory for state recovery after hardware reboots. Experimental results on an ARM Cortex-A53 platform demonstrate a 35% reduction in maximum heap usage and a 40% improvement in end-to-end transaction latency. This framework provides a scalable, sustainable blueprint for maintaining transactional integrity on legacy hardware in the 2026 IoT landscape.

</details>


### [10] [AI-Driven Scientific Computing Workflows: A Systems Review of Orchestration, Execution, Reproducibility and Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21162)
*Jamie J. Alnasir*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文从系统视角综述了AI与科学计算工作流的耦合，提出从计算阶段到协同自适应工作流控制的连续谱，并围绕五大系统关注点组织需求，总结了代表性系统与评估维度，指出若干开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI日益嵌入融合仿真、数据处理、优化、可视化与实验观测的科学计算工作流，习得模型可作为显式组件、保持持久状态并在自适应场景中影响后续计算。已有工作分别刻画了科学工作流管理系统、动态与受引导工作流、AI-HPC耦合模式及机器学习生命周期，但这些领域常被分开讨论，缺乏统一的系统视角。

Method: 采用系统视角的综述方法，将上述分离领域整合起来。区分传统科学工作流、机器学习流水线、AI耦合的高性能计算（HPC）工作流以及更广泛的自动化研究工作流，并提出描述AI参与深度的连续谱（从计算阶段到协同自适应工作流控制）。将相关系统需求归纳为五类关注点进行组织。

Result: 围绕五个关注点组织系统需求：控制与编排；计算与执行；数据与模型状态；可复现性与溯源；治理与保障。代表性系统与应用包括AI引导的分子模拟、药物与材料发现、仿真-代理模型耦合以及分布式自驱动实验室。工作流级评估从科学进展、执行成本、数据移动、资源使用、韧性与决策可追溯性等维度展开。

Conclusion: 识别出若干开放问题：动态工作流表示、状态感知恢复、异构调度、可互操作数据平面、模型介导的决策溯源以及可复现的自适应执行。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded within scientific computing workflows that combine simulation, data processing, optimisation, visualisation and experimental or observational components. Learned models may serve as explicit workflow components, retain persistent state and, in adaptive settings, influence subsequent computation. Existing work has characterised scientific workflow management systems, dynamic and steered workflows, AI--HPC coupling motifs and the machine-learning lifecycle, although these areas are often discussed separately. This review brings them together from a systems perspective. We distinguish conventional scientific workflows, machine-learning pipelines, AI-coupled high-performance computing (HPC) workflows and broader automated research workflows, and propose a continuum describing the depth of AI participation from a computational stage to co-adaptive workflow control. The associated systems requirements are organised around five concerns: control and orchestration; compute and execution; data and model state; reproducibility and provenance; and governance and assurance. Representative systems and applications include AI-steered molecular simulation, drug and materials discovery, simulation--surrogate coupling and distributed self-driving laboratories. Workflow-level evaluation is considered in terms of scientific progress, execution cost, data movement, resource use, resilience and decision traceability. We conclude by identifying open problems in dynamic workflow representation, state-aware recovery, heterogeneous scheduling, interoperable data planes, model-mediated decision provenance and reproducible adaptive execution.

</details>


### [11] [Brain API: An Intent-Aware Control Plane for Policy-Governed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21299)
*Alexander Chernov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Brain API——一种面向策略治理的 agentic 系统的意图感知控制平面，核心贡献是"决策制品"：对"意图如何转化为可执行计划"的持久、可版本化、可审计记录。原型在 OPA Gatekeeper 约束库的 42 个可编码案例上全部与官方判决一致（19 允许、23 拒绝），并在 Cedar 示例策略上暴露出三个模型缺陷（含会导致授权反转的默认允许假设）。评估仅覆盖策略过滤与选择，上下文信号与排序仍为设计主张，负载下的决策延迟未量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云与分布式系统的控制抽象以资源为中心（服务、部署、网络流、执行图），而 agentic 与工具增强系统已把应用逻辑推向意图驱动、自适应执行。但现有控制平面、工作流引擎与服务网格缺乏意图级决策治理的抽象：无法把高层目标作为一等控制对象，无法对"意图→执行计划"的映射施加策略，也无法产出"为何选择某条执行路径而非其他替代方案"的可审计记录，导致控制逻辑散落在应用代码中，系统脆弱、不透明、难以治理。

Method: 提出 Brain API，即面向策略治理的 agentic 系统的意图感知控制平面。中央贡献是决策制品（decision artifact）：持久、可版本化、可审计地记录意图如何变为可执行计划，包含适用了哪些策略、评估了哪些能力、拒绝了哪些替代方案及其原因。动机用例是 agentic 数据集——在驻留、合规与成本约束下作为受策略治理的能力参与。实现决策层原型，并用两个并非作者编写的外部策略语料库进行评估：OPA Gatekeeper 约束库（其已公布的判决）与 Cedar 示例策略（通过与其参考实现做差分测试来标注）。

Result: 在 OPA Gatekeeper 约束库上，原型与库自身公布的判决在 42 个可编码案例中全部一致（19 个允许、23 个拒绝）。在 Cedar 示例策略上，这一刻意选择的不相似领域暴露出模型中的三个缺陷，其中包括一个会导致所有授权策略被反转的默认允许假设。评估范围覆盖策略过滤与选择；上下文信号与排序仍属设计主张，负载下的决策延迟尚未量化。

Conclusion: 决策制品为意图级控制提供了可审计、可版本化的治理抽象，并在两个外部策略语料库上获得初步验证；但当前评估仅限策略过滤与选择，跨域迁移暴露的建模缺陷（如默认允许假设）以及上下文信号、排序与负载性能仍待进一步检验。

Abstract: Contemporary cloud and distributed systems expose control through resource-centric abstractions: services, deployments, network flows, execution graphs. Agentic and tool-augmented systems have meanwhile shifted application logic toward intent-driven, adaptive execution. Existing control planes, workflow engines and service meshes lack abstractions for intent-level decision governance: they cannot represent high-level goals as first-class control objects, cannot enforce policy over the mapping from intent to execution plan, and cannot produce auditable records of why one execution path was chosen over its alternatives. Control logic is therefore embedded in application code, leaving systems brittle, opaque and hard to govern.
  We propose Brain API, an intent-aware control plane for policy-governed agentic systems. Its central contribution is the decision artifact: a durable, versioned, auditable record of how an intent became an executable plan, capturing which policies applied, which capabilities were evaluated, which alternatives were rejected, and why. A motivating use case is agentic datasets: datasets participating as policy-governed capabilities under residency, compliance and cost constraints.
  We evaluate a prototype of the decision layer against two external policy corpora we did not author. On the OPA Gatekeeper constraint library it agrees with the library's own published verdicts on 42 of 42 encodable cases, 19 admit and 23 deny. On Cedar example policies, labeled by differential testing against its reference implementation, a deliberately dissimilar domain exposed three defects in our model, including a default-allow assumption that would have inverted every authorization policy. The evaluation covers policy filtering and selection; context-signal and ranking remain design claims, and decision latency under load is unquantified.

</details>


### [12] [TokaGLINT: A Scalable GPU-Tailored Implicit Solver for Full 3D Tokamak Electromagnetic Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21366)
*Zifan Yang,Haoyuan Zhang,Jialin Li,Wu Yuan,Xiazhen Liu,Jian Zhang,Jianyuan Xiao,Shan Liang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TokaGLINT：面向全三维托卡马克电磁场计算的GPU加速隐式求解器，通过分层区域分解与快速精确局部求解器协同设计，在超万块GPU上实现90.1%弱扩展与53.9%强扩展效率，单节点较BiCGStab基线加速2.67倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向全三维托卡马克仿真中的电磁场计算，需要支撑大规模并行GPU高效计算，并实现高保真、长时程建模，现有方法在硬件利用率与长时间稳定性方面存在不足。

Method: 提出TokaGLINT隐式求解器：协同设计分层区域分解与快速精确局部求解器，分层划分匹配卡内细粒度子域；采用面向曲线坐标辛CN-FDTD离散三维麦克斯韦方程的张量化求解器；配合面向卡内多子域结构的自动算子融合与批处理，通过离散变换解耦未知量，利用张量结构计算提升硬件利用率，并保持辛离散的长时间稳定性。

Result: 电磁场求解可扩展至超10,000块GPU，弱扩展效率90.1%、强扩展效率53.9%；单节点相比未预条件BiCGStab基线（HIP版HYPRE）获得2.67倍加速；在SymPIC等离子体模拟代码中的EAST托卡马克仿真中得到验证。

Conclusion: TokaGLINT验证了大规模GPU并行下高效、稳定的三维托卡马克电磁场隐式求解可行性，可支撑高保真长时程等离子体建模。

Abstract: We introduce TokaGLINT, a GPU-accelerated implicit solver for electromagnetic field computations in full 3D tokamak simulations, aimed at efficient large-scale parallel GPU computing. Its central innovation lies in the co-design of hierarchical domain decomposition and a fast exact local solver, where hierarchical partitioning is tailored to match fine-grained intra-card subdomains and exploit the tensor-based solver dedicated to curvilinear-coordinate symplectic CN-FDTD-discretized 3D Maxwell equations. Backed by automated operator fusion and batching customized for the intra-card multi-subdomain structure, the solver decouples unknowns through discrete transformations and leverages tensor-structured computations to achieve high hardware utilization, while preserving the long-time stability characteristic of symplectic discretizations. TokaGLINT scales the electromagnetic field solve beyond 10,000 GPUs, achieving 90.1% weak and 53.9% strong scaling efficiency, while delivering a 2.67X single-node speedup over an unpreconditioned BiCGStab baseline (HIP-enabled HYPRE). It is validated in EAST tokamak simulations within the SymPIC plasma simulation code, enabling high-fidelity long-duration modeling.

</details>


### [13] [Weave: Fine-Grained Dynamic SM Scheduling in an MoE Megakernel for Compute-Communication Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21483)
*Ziyu Huang,Yangjie Zhou,Chenhao Zhu,Zihan Liu,Jinyu Liu,Shulai Zhang,Xingxun Tang,Hongzhe Yan,Xinhao Luo,Minyi Guo,Xiu Lin,Yinghao Yu,Guodong Yang,Liping Zhang,Shixuan Sun,Jingwen Leng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Weave提出首个细粒度动态SM调度的MoE重叠系统，通过运行时按层、按GPU划分通信与计算SM并结合时空调度器，在4xH100上实现MoE层2.89x、端到端1.33x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家并行下的MoE推理中，dispatch和combine通信开销大。现有通信-计算重叠系统通过划分SM来分别执行通信和计算，但仍存在两方面资源浪费：空间上，最优SM划分依赖每层路由结果，随层与GPU变化，固定策略会导致NVLink带宽或计算吞吐的浪费；时间上，复杂的数据依赖引入气泡使SM空闲。

Method: Weave在持久化megakernel内运行轻量级代价模型。路由完成后即可预知每层的通信与计算量：空间调度器将SM划分为通信worker和计算worker以匹配通信/计算吞吐比；时间调度器协调两组worker以最小化SM空闲。

Result: 在4x H100 SXM GPU上，针对六种主流MoE模型，Weave相比五个最先进基线取得几何平均2.89x的MoE层加速和1.33x的端到端加速。

Conclusion: Weave是首个执行细粒度动态SM调度的MoE重叠系统，能够按层、按GPU根据运行时路由结果自适应调度，有效缓解空间和时间两个维度的资源浪费。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference under expert parallelism (EP) turns each MoE layer into a distributed computation with costly dispatch and combine communication. State-of-the-art systems reduce this cost through communication-computation overlap, splitting the GPU's SMs for communication and computation respectively. However, this approach still leaves GPU resources wasted along two dimensions. Spatially, the best SM split is determined by each layer's routing result and varies across layers and GPUs, so fixed policies mismatch the workload and waste either NVLink bandwidth or compute throughput. Temporally, complex MoE data dependencies introduce bubbles that leave SMs idle.
  We present Weave, to our knowledge the first MoE overlap system that performs fine-grained dynamic SM scheduling - deciding per layer and per GPU by routing results at runtime. Once routing completes, each layer's communication and computation volumes become known; Weave exploits this predictability through a lightweight cost model running inside the persistent megakernel: a spatial scheduler partitions SMs into communication workers and computation workers to match the communication/computation throughput ratio, and a temporal scheduler coordinates the two worker groups to minimize SM idleness. On 4x H100 SXM GPUs across six mainstream MoE models, Weave achieves a 2.89x geometric-mean MoE-layer speedup and a 1.33x geometric-mean end-to-end speedup over five state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [14] [HyperParallel-FSDP: Topology-Aware Fully Sharded Training with Layout-Driven Muon on Ascend SuperPods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21594)
*Mo Sun,Yifan Yao,Yanwei Liu,Luobin Liu,Zhenzhang Yang,Kaisheng Wang,Xiangyu Meng,Chen Li,Xizheng Pang,Huilan Li,Xinglei Xu,Yushi Cui,Xinyao Lin,Kaiqi Chen,Jie Zhang,Zeke Wang,Teng Su*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 HyperParallel-FSDP，通过将分片语义提升到 autograd 之上的张量 API 边界，实现双模式 DTensor 执行、拓扑感知 FSDP 和布局驱动的分布式 Muon，在 Atlas 900 A3 上显著提升训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有声明式 SPMD 编程（如 PyTorch DTensor）在 autograd 之下分派每个算子，导致重复分派与元数据开销，且缺乏低成本的端到端验证路径；同时 FSDP 依赖显式参数打包/解包、Muon 的整矩阵正交化与参数分片冲突，均不匹配两层超节点拓扑。

Method: 核心观察是分布式张量只需在 autograd 之上的张量 API 边界表达分片语义，使微分和算子作用于普通张量。基于此提出三项设计：(1) 双模式 DTensor 执行，用同一分片计划支持生产模式（一次性布局解析、无稳态分派开销）与验证模式（端到端元数据传播、快速失败检查、梯度等价测试）；(2) 拓扑感知 FSDP，含超节点内零拷贝集合通信、超节点间融合规约、跨层反向流水以避免等待慢链路；(3) 布局驱动分布式 Muon，含分片推导的通信组、去重正交化、形状融合的 Newton-Schulz 迭代。

Result: 在 Atlas 900 A3 SuperPoD 上，HyperParallel-FSDP 从 16 die 扩展至 384 卡（768 rank），为 505B 参数 MoE 维持 421k tokens/s，FSDP 通信仅占步时 2.9%。相比 PyTorch FSDP2 平均步时降低 29.7%，相比 Megatron DDP 降低 25.5%，1000 步 Pearson 相关性高于 0.999997。分布式 Muon 相比竞争系统提升 profiler 步时 5.4-16.0%。

Conclusion: HyperParallel-FSDP 证明了将分片语义上移至 autograd 之上可有效消除稳态分派开销并支持廉价验证，配合拓扑感知通信与布局驱动的分布式优化器，在大规模 MoE 训练中取得显著加速，具有良好的可扩展性。源码已开源。

Abstract: Declarative SPMD programming uses tensor sharding descriptions to drive distributed execution, separating parallelization from model code. However, the evaluated PyTorch DTensor stack dispatches every operator below autograd, incurring repeated dispatch and metadata costs, while lacking an inexpensive end-to-end validation path. Existing FSDP and distributed Muon implementations also mismatch two-tier supernode topologies: FSDP relies on explicit parameter packing and unpacking, and Muon's whole-matrix orthogonalization conflicts with parameter sharding.
  We observe that distributed tensors need only express sharding semantics at the tensor API boundary above autograd, allowing differentiation and kernels to operate on plain tensors. Based on this insight, we present HyperParallel-FSDP, featuring: (1) dual-mode DTensor execution, using one sharding plan for both a production mode with one-time layout resolution and no steady-state dispatch overhead, and a validation mode with end-to-end metadata propagation, fail-fast checks, and gradient-equivalence testing; (2) topology-aware FSDP, with zero-copy intra-supernode collectives, fused inter-supernode reduction, and a cross-layer backward pipeline that avoids waits on slow links; and (3) layout-driven distributed Muon, with sharding-derived communication groups, deduplicated orthogonalization, and shape-fused Newton-Schulz iterations.
  On Atlas 900 A3 SuperPoD, HyperParallel-FSDP scales from 16 dies to 384 cards (768 ranks), sustaining 421k tokens/s for a 505B-parameter MoE while FSDP communication uses 2.9% of step time. It reduces mean step time by 29.7% versus PyTorch FSDP2 and 25.5% versus Megatron DDP, with Pearson correlation above 0.999997 over 1,000 steps. Distributed Muon improves profiler step time by 5.4-16.0% over competing systems. Source code is available at https://atomgit.com/mindspore/hyper-parallel.

</details>


### [15] [PoVD: Efficient Consensus Protocol based on Verifiable Delay Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21627)
*Rui Jiang,Xintong Ling,Bin Cao,Jiaheng Wang,Xiqi Gao,Zhi Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于可验证延迟函数（VDF）的轻量级共识协议PoVD，旨在降低共识的计算与通信开销，并在较弱网络假设下实现比PoW更低的分叉率和相同吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共识机制通常存在计算成本高或通信开销大的问题，难以适应资源受限节点和带宽受限网络，因此需要一种更轻量且高效的共识协议。

Method: 提出基于可验证延迟函数（VDF）的共识协议PoVD，给出包括区块挖掘和验证规则在内的详细协议设计，并展示其可根据网络状况灵活调整区块时间分布；同时通过数学证明分析其分叉率与吞吐量表现，并进行实验验证。

Result: 数学证明表明，在相对较弱的网络假设下，PoVD能在保持与PoW相同吞吐量的同时实现更低的分叉率；实验验证PoVD具有较低的计算和通信复杂度，并能提升区块链一致性。

Conclusion: PoVD是一种适合资源受限节点和带宽受限网络的轻量级共识协议，能够在降低开销的同时改善区块链一致性。

Abstract: Consensus protocols ensure the robustness and scalability of blockchains and decentralized applications built on them. However, existing consensus mechanisms often impose high computational cost or require heavy communication overhead. To address these challenges, we propose proof of verifiable delay (PoVD), a lightweight consensus protocol based on the verifiable delay function (VDF). We present the detailed protocol of PoVD, including the block mining and verification rules, and illustrate how PoVD can flexibly adjust the block time distribution according to the network condition. Through mathematical proof, we point out that, under a relatively weak network assumption, PoVD can achieve a lower fork rate than PoW while maintaining the same throughput. Our experiments verify that PoVD exhibits low computation and communication complexity and can also improve the blockchain consistency, making it particularly suitable for resource-constrained nodes and bandwidth-limited networks.

</details>


### [16] [Distributed Balanced Butterfly Counting in Signed Bipartite Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21848)
*Kiran Mekala,Apurba Das,Suman Banerjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出分布式算法D-BBC，基于MPI+TBB混合框架实现平衡蝴蝶（balanced butterfly）计数，在15个真实数据集上相比串行BB2K平均加速1321倍，相比多核M-BBC加速16.2倍，相比分布式基线S-Monarch最高加速23.58倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡蝴蝶是分析符号二分图的基础原语，为聚类系数和社区结构等高阶结构性质研究提供基础。然而现有方法主要依赖串行算法进行平衡蝴蝶计数，在大规模图上效率低下，因此需要高效的分布式解决方案。

Method: 提出分布式算法D-BBC，采用MPI+TBB混合框架：利用MPI实现进程间通信，利用Intel TBB实现节点内并行。在15个真实世界数据集上进行实验评估。

Result: 在单节点分布式系统上，D-BBC相比串行BB2K平均加速1321倍，相比多核M-BBC加速16.2倍；在端到端执行时间上，相比分布式基线S-Monarch最高加速23.58倍。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了所提分布式方法的高效性，以及其在大规模二分图上实现高性能符号模体分析的潜力。

Abstract: The balanced butterfly is a fundamental primitive for analyzing signed bipartite graphs and provides a basis for studying higher-order structural properties, such as clustering coefficients and community structure. Despite its importance, existing approaches primarily rely on serial algorithms for balanced butterfly counting, which become inefficient on large-scale graphs. To address this limitation, we propose a distributed algorithm, D-BBC, based on a hybrid MPI+TBB framework that exploits MPI for inter-process communication and Intel TBB for intra-node parallelism. We conduct an experimental assessment of the proposed approach across 15 real-world datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that, on a single-node distributed system, D-BBC achieves average speedups of 1321x and 16.2x over the serial BB2K and multi-core M-BBC implementations, respectively. Furthermore, D-BBC achieves a maximum speedup of 23.58x over the distributed baseline S-Monarch in terms of end-to-end execution time. These results demonstrate the efficiency of the proposed distributed approach and its potential to enable high- performance signed motif analysis on large-scale bipartite graphs.

</details>


### [17] [dSTAR: Straggler Tolerant and Byzantine Resilient Distributed SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07151)
*Jiahe Yan,Pratik Chaudhari,Leonard Kleinrock*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出轻量高效的分布式SGD方法dSTAR，通过选取前k个响应节点并用集成中位数偏差过滤，兼顾抗straggler与抗Byzantine攻击，理论证明其(α, f)-Byzantine弹性与线性收敛，实验保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式模型训练面临straggler效应与Byzantine攻击两大挑战，需要在网络和系统故障下实现及时可靠的梯度聚合。现有Byzantine弹性方法在攻击下精度下降可达40-50%，亟需更鲁棒且轻量的方案。

Method: dSTAR选择性聚合梯度：仅收集最先响应的前k个worker的更新，基于集成中位数（ensemble median）计算偏差并进行过滤，从而同时缓解straggler影响并抵御Byzantine对手。

Result: 理论证明dSTAR具有(α, f)-Byzantine弹性并达到线性收敛速率；多场景实证显示其持续保持高精度，优于在攻击下精度下降40-50%的其他Byzantine弹性方法。

Conclusion: dSTAR是一种适用于同时存在straggler延迟与Byzantine故障的分布式环境的鲁棒训练方案。

Abstract: Distributed model training needs to be adapted to challenges such as the straggler effect and Byzantine attacks. When coordinating the training process with multiple computing nodes, ensuring timely and reliable gradient aggregation amidst network and system malfunctions is essential. To tackle these issues, we propose \textit{dSTAR}, a lightweight and efficient approach for distributed stochastic gradient descent (SGD) that enhances robustness and convergence. \textit{dSTAR} selectively aggregates gradients by collecting updates from the first \(k\) workers to respond, filtering them based on deviations calculated using an ensemble median. This method not only mitigates the impact of stragglers but also fortifies the model against Byzantine adversaries. We theoretically establish that \textit{dSTAR} is (\(α, f\))-Byzantine resilient and achieves a linear convergence rate. Empirical evaluations across various scenarios demonstrate that \textit{dSTAR} consistently maintains high accuracy, outperforming other Byzantine-resilient methods that often suffer up to a 40-50\% accuracy drop under attack. Our results highlight \textit{dSTAR} as a robust solution for training models in distributed environments prone to both straggler delays and Byzantine faults.

</details>

### [18] [Helpful but Fallible: Developer Experiences of AI Tools Under a Coordinated Industrial Roll-out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20977)
*Andreas Bexell,Rushali Gupta,Lo Gullstrand Heander,Emma Söderberg,Per Runeson,Sigrid Eldh,Wei Shi,Konstantin Malysh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过对瑞典某大型电信公司12名软件开发者的半结构化访谈，考察AI开发工具在协调式推广中的开发者体验及其对职业变化的预期，发现管理者与开发者之间存在期望差距，且感知风险被严重低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI开发工具正被产业界大规模采用，并寄予提升生产力的厚望，但开发者对这些工具推广过程的真实体验研究不足。现有证据主要来自工具评估、生产力指标和问卷调研，缺乏对正在进行中的协调式推广的定性、现场研究，导致组织在预算投入、员工评估和实践调整上依赖片面信息。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，聚焦瑞典一家大型电信公司的AI开发工具协调式推广。对分布于三个站点的12名软件专业人员开展半结构化访谈，使用过程编码和主题分析处理数据，并以扩展技术接受模型（TAM2）作为事后分析视角进行解读。

Result: 研究在使用场景、生产力、挫折感及工具局限等方面的发现，印证了此前的问卷研究结论。此外，访谈揭示出管理层与开发者之间的期望差距，该差距对应TAM2中的主观规范与自愿性构念；同时发现参与者高度重视感知风险，而TAM2等接受模型并未涵盖这一因素。

Conclusion: AI开发工具表现为有用但易出错的助手，其价值受组织期望、系统规模和开发者技能共同塑造。研究提示，现有技术接受模型需要纳入感知风险维度，组织在推广AI开发工具时也应正视管理者与开发者之间的期望落差。

Abstract: AI-enabled software development tools (AI-devtools) are being industrially adopted under strong expectations of productivity gains, yet developers' experiences of such roll-outs are underexplored. Organizations commit budgets, evaluate staff, and revise practice on a partial picture, since the evidence base is mainly tool evaluations, productivity metrics, and surveys, with few qualitative in-situ accounts of ongoing, coordinated roll-outs. We report a case study of a coordinated roll-out of AI-devtools at a large Swedish telecommunications company, investigating how developers experience the roll-out and how they anticipate their profession will change. We conducted semi-structured interviews with 12 software professionals across three sites, analyzed with process coding and thematic analysis, and interpreted through the extended Technology Acceptance Model (TAM2) as a post-hoc analytical lens. Our findings on use cases, productivity, frustrations, and tool limitations corroborate prior survey work. Beyond corroboration, the interviews surface a management-developer expectation gap that maps onto the TAM2 constructs of subjective norm and voluntariness, and show that participants weigh perceived risk heavily, a factor that TAM2 and similar acceptance models do not represent. AI-devtools emerge as helpful but fallible assistants whose value is shaped by organizational expectations, system scale, and developers' skills.

</details>


### [19] [Embedding Drift in Code Vulnerability Models Under Intended Behaviour-Preserving Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21203)
*Hasti Ghaneshirazi,Tahsin Reza,Ladan Tahvildari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文研究代码语义保持变换（注释删除、不可达代码插入、变量重命名、循环重写）会导致冻结代码嵌入跨越分类器决策边界，使漏洞预测翻转。作者用15,000个C/C++函数（7,500个漏洞-修补对）在四个变异轨迹下评估鲁棒性，并提出一种仅训练的防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义中性的代码编辑（如删除注释、插入不可达代码、重命名变量、重写循环）不应改变模型的安全判断，但这类保持程序行为的编辑可能使冻结代码嵌入跨越分类器决策边界，导致原本正确检测的漏洞被预测为良性，因此需要研究并缓解这种预测不稳定性。

Method: 使用Big-Vul数据集中15,000个C/C++函数（7,500个漏洞-修补对）。对漏洞与良性样本施加四种变异轨迹：注释删除、不可达代码插入、变量重命名、循环重写，以及组合所有变换的设置。采用冻结的microsoft/codebert-base嵌入配合六种下游分类器，评估漏洞预测鲁棒性，并提出一种仅训练的防御：在保留漏洞类别信息的同时，将干净与变异表示投影得更近。

Result: 组合变异下基线脆弱翻转率（VFR）为35.55%–42.15%。所提防御将平均共同集VFR从22.60%降至11.32%，平均变异准确率从55.33%提升至57.10%。对逻辑回归，VFR下降17.29个百分点，但良性→漏洞翻转增加11.95个百分点。

Conclusion: 该防御提升了对语义保持代码变换的鲁棒性，但引入了假阳性权衡，并未完全消除预测不稳定性。

Abstract: Code edits that preserve program behaviour can shift frozen code embeddings across classifier decision boundaries, causing correctly detected vulnerabilities to be predicted as benign. Such instability is problematic because semantically neutral changes, including comment removal, insertion of unreachable code, variable renaming, and loop rewriting, should not alter a model's security judgment. We investigate this issue using 15,000 C/C++ functions organized as 7,500 vulnerable-patched pairs from the Big-Vul dataset. Four mutation tracks are applied to both vulnerable and benign samples: comment removal, unreachable code insertion, variable renaming, loop rewriting, and a combined setting that applies all transformations. Using frozen microsoft/codebert-base embeddings with six downstream classifiers, we evaluate the robustness of vulnerability predictions and introduce a train-only defence that projects clean and mutated representations closer together while preserving vulnerability-class information. Under combined mutations, the baseline Vulnerable Flip Rate (VFR) ranges from 35.55% to 42.15%. The proposed defence reduces the mean common-set VFR from 22.60% to 11.32% and improves mean mutated accuracy from 55.33% to 57.10%. For Logistic Regression, VFR decreases by 17.29 percentage points, although benign-to-vulnerable flips increase by 11.95 percentage points. These results indicate that the defence improves robustness to semantics-preserving code transformations but introduces a false-positive trade-off and does not fully eliminate prediction instability.

</details>


### [20] [License Compliance in Open Source Cybersecurity Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21218)
*Ahmed Shah,Selman Selman,Ibrahim Abualhaol*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对200多个开源网络安全项目进行初步许可证分析，识别最常用许可证类型与语言，并发现宽松许可项目中被限制性许可代码污染及版权归属缺失的案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全软件开发者常将开源包引入商业产品，需确认其许可证是否宽松以支持商业继承与再分发。但开源包许可证可能被限制性许可代码悄然污染，导致商业产品面临高额整改成本、声誉损害与法律费用风险，因此有必要调查此类污染现象。

Method: 对200多个开源网络安全项目进行初步分析，识别最常用的许可证类型与编程语言，并检测宽松许可项目中被限制性许可材料污染的迹象，同时统计版权归属缺失情况。

Result: 在宽松许可的开源项目中发现了限制性许可证污染案例，并且发现相当高比例的代码缺少版权归属信息。

Conclusion: 研究结果为管理者与开发者理解污染成因、为开源社区改进知识产权保护（在代码中加入许可证与版权信息）、以及为创业者理解开源网络安全领域的许可与污染问题及其对架构决策的影响提供依据。

Abstract: Developers of cybersecurity software often include and rely upon open source software packages in their commercial software products. Before open source code is absorbed into a proprietary product, developers must check the package license to see if the project is permissively licensed, thereby allowing for commercial-friendly inheritance and redistribution. However, there is a risk that the open source package license could be inaccurate due to being silently contaminated with restrictively licensed open source code that may prohibit the sale or confidentiality of commercial derivative work. Contamination of commercial products could lead to expensive remediation costs, damage to the company's reputation, and costly legal fees. In this article, we report on our preliminary analysis of more than 200 open source cybersecurity projects to identify the most frequently used license types and languages and to look for evidence of permissively licensed open source projects that are likely contaminated by restrictive licensed material (i.e., containing commercial-unfriendly code). Our analysis identified restrictive license contamination cases occurring in permissively licensed open source projects. Furthermore, we found a high proportion of code that lacked copyright attribution. We expect that the results of this study will: i) provide managers and developers with an understanding of how contamination can occur, ii) provide open source communities with an understanding on how they can better protect their intellectual property by including licenses and copyright information in their code, and iii) provide entrepreneurs with an understanding of the open source cybersecurity domain in terms of licensing and contamination and how they affect decisions about cybersecurity software architectures.

</details>


### [21] [What Stops a Small Language Model From Driving a Database Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21341)
*Cevheri Bozoglan,Yusuf Gundogdu,Abdullah Kaya,Koray Sirin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过11天、8,199次运行的日志分析发现，小型开源模型在智能体数据库任务中的失败多数并非推理能力不足，而是工具调用与服务器端的机械性缺陷所致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界普遍假设小规模开源模型因推理能力不足而无法胜任智能体化的数据库工作。作者试图用一个真实生产系统来检验这一假设，并探究失败究竟源于模型能力还是系统其他环节。

Method: 驱动开源SQL客户端的智能体模式，本地部署39个开源权重模型并设一个托管对照模型，覆盖六类任务界面，持续11天，共8,199次运行、110,711条账本事件、14,008次被拒工具调用。对模型归因的损失进行聚类重采样，并补记原本未记录的拒绝代码与模型参数。

Result: 2,100次归因于模型的损失中，1,590次（75.7%）来自至少调用过一次工具的运行，该多数在99.7%的聚类重采样中成立。其中传输类（用了工具却未产出交付物）最多，占36.2%；能力类（完全未调用工具）最少，占17.3%。捕获被拒参数暴露出5个服务器缺陷；5项服务器改动在未触及模型、提示或采样设置的情况下，使6个模型在30格中移动6至21格。另发现一个混杂因素：无上下文上限时，一个7.1GB模型被允许使用完整262,144 token窗口并在64GB机器上占用51GB内存。

Conclusion: 小型开源模型的智能体失败主要是工具调用与服务器实现问题，而非单纯的推理能力缺失；生产日志若不记录拒绝代码与参数，会长期掩盖这些缺陷。作者发布了语料、评分器和可复现全部数字的验证器。

Abstract: Small open-weight language models are assumed to fail at agentic database work because they lack the reasoning capacity for it. We test that against a production system. Over eleven days we drove the agent mode of an open-source SQL client with 39 open-weight models served locally and one hosted control, across six task surfaces: 8,199 runs, 110,711 ledger events, 14,008 refused tool calls. Of the 2,100 model-attributed agent-mode losses, 1,590, or 75.7%, came from runs that had invoked at least one tool. That majority is what survives resampling models rather than runs: it holds in 99.7% of clustered resamples and in 15 of the 22 models with at least twenty losses. Within it, transport, a run that used the tools and never got a deliverable through, is the largest class at 36.2% and capability, a run that invoked no tool at all, the smallest at 17.3%; we report that ordering as a property of this corpus rather than a general finding, since clustered by model it holds in only 74.5% of resamples. Transport failures decompose into a few mechanical argument shapes. Production ledgers record refusal codes and never the model's arguments, so these were invisible for ten days; capturing them exposed five server defects, one of which demanded a field on one tool, forbade it on the sibling that composed it, then failed the run for its absence. Five server changes, touching no model, prompt or sampling setting, moved six models by 6 to 21 cells out of 30. We also report a confound we believe affects published local-model benchmarks, ours included: with no context cap, one 7.1 GB model was admitted at its full 262,144-token window and held 51 GB on a 64 GB machine, producing runs indistinguishable in any ordinary log from a model timing out. The corpus, the scorer and a verifier that regenerates every figure are released.

</details>


### [22] [Cross-Platform vs Native Mobile Development: An Empirical Study of Software Quality Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21544)
*Alexandru Ilovan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对比同一植物管理应用的5种实现（原生iOS、原生Android、Flutter、React Native、Kotlin Multiplatform，共8个可执行变体），基于ISO/IEC 25010考察时间行为、实现规模、源码组织与渲染响应性，结论是不存在普遍最优技术，各方案的优劣取决于质量属性、工作负载、平台与测量边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨平台移动框架虽承诺代码复用、缩短交付周期与降低实现成本，但其相对于原生开发的取舍难以客观评估；既有对比多依赖简化应用、不一致的功能集或狭窄的指标集，缺乏统一基准。

Method: 构建同一植物管理应用的5种实现（原生iOS、原生Android、Flutter、React Native、Kotlin Multiplatform），跨平台方案同时面向Android与iOS，共8个可执行变体。所有实现共享相同领域、后端服务、功能需求与基准契约；数据集含每变体2,000次完整运行，覆盖鉴权与缓存检索、图像传输与解码、列表渲染与滚动、本地同步、媒体上传；后端准备与框架专用UI驱动与主客户端工作流分开分析。评估框架以ISO/IEC 25010为指导。

Result: 原生在两个操作系统上均记录了最低的非UI客户端小计，Kotlin Multiplatform是最接近的跨平台实现；各任务的操作与UI包装层排名存在差异。跨平台方案合计编写的移动端源码少于两个原生应用之和；源码清单显示文件大小、组织方式与依赖使用上存在不同模式。

Conclusion: 研究并非给出普遍最优的技术选择，而是表明每种方案的优势与成本均取决于具体质量属性、工作负载、平台及测量边界。

Abstract: Cross-platform mobile frameworks promise code reuse, shorter delivery cycles, and lower implementation effort, but their trade-offs relative to native development remain difficult to assess objectively. Many comparisons rely on simplified applications, inconsistent feature sets, or a narrow set of metrics. This paper compares five implementations of the same plant-management application: native iOS, native Android, Flutter, React Native, and Kotlin Multiplatform. The shared approaches target both Android and iOS, yielding eight executable variants. Guided by ISO/IEC 25010, the study examines time behavior, implementation footprint, source-code organization, and observable rendering responsiveness.
  All implementations share the same domain, backend services, functional requirements, and benchmark contract. The supplied dataset contains 2,000 completed runs per variant and covers authenticated and cached retrieval, image transfer and decoding, list rendering and scrolling, local synchronization, and media upload. Backend preparation and framework-specific UI drivers are analyzed separately from the primary client workflow.
  Native records the lowest non-UI client subtotal on both operating systems, with Kotlin Multiplatform the closest cross-platform implementation, while operation and UI-wrapper rankings vary by task. The shared approaches contain less authored mobile source than the native applications combined, and the source inventory shows different patterns of file size, organization, and dependency use. Rather than identifying a universally superior technology, the study shows that each approach's advantages and costs depend on the quality attribute, workload, platform, and measurement boundary.

</details>


### [23] [GameLogicBench: Evaluating Coding Agents on Runtime Game Logic with Tick-Level State Assertions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21562)
*Xinyu Che,Yunfei Ge,Shihao Li,Yanchen Liu,Hang Yan,Xinping Lei,Yanghai Wang,Zixuan Dong,Yifan Yao,Qianqian Xie,Letian Zhu,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 GameLogicBench，一个包含 72 个 Godot 游戏逻辑任务、403 个场景、1451 个测试用例的基准，可在每个仿真 tick 检查游戏规则，并确保评估结果可复现。最佳模型仅解决 52.78% 的任务，任务规模越大表现越差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏开发中智能体需实现游戏规则，且游戏即使在运行中违反规则也可能以有效状态结束，因此仅靠回放示例、评分视频或让另一模型评判的结果，无法在多样场景中贯穿执行过程检查规则并保证裁决完全可复现。现有基准缺乏这一能力。

Method: 构建 GameLogicBench：72 个 Godot 项目中的游戏逻辑任务，含 403 个手工设计场景，通过种子参数变化生成 1451 个测试用例；自动评估器在每个仿真 tick 检查规则。为确保评估行为而非实现选择，评估器需接受不同的正确实现，同时拒绝变异体（移除某一必需能力的实现）。任务覆盖孤立机制、多系统交互和仓库级功能三个层次。

Result: 在 20 种语言模型与脚手架组合中，最佳单次运行解决 52.78% 的任务。在 Claude Code 下，全部十二个模型随任务范围从孤立机制扩展到多系统交互、再到仓库级功能，解决的任务数均减少。智能体在仓库级任务上更频繁地检查代码、调用更多工具。多数失败提交可运行，但部分必需游戏行为实现错误。使用变异体验证与不验证的评估器版本对比显示，未经验证时错误的智能体提交会通过。另发现网络开放时智能体会从公开仓库复制代码。

Conclusion: 可靠的评估既取决于测试拒绝什么，也取决于智能体可访问哪些外部代码。GameLogicBench 通过 tick 级规则检查、变异体验证和场景多样化，提供了可复现且能区分行为正确性的游戏逻辑评估方案。

Abstract: Coding agents can modify and test code across large software projects. Game development is a domain where agents must implement gameplay rules. A game can end in a valid state even after violating its rules during the run. Current game-development benchmarks replay fixed examples, score videos, or ask another model to judge the result. However, no existing benchmark checks game rules throughout execution across varied evaluator-selected scenarios while ensuring exactly reproducible verdicts. We introduce GameLogicBench, a benchmark of 72 gameplay-logic tasks in Godot projects. An automated evaluator checks each game's rules at every simulation tick. Across 403 hand-designed scenarios, seeded parameter variations produce 1,451 test cases. To ensure that the evaluator measures behavior rather than implementation choice, it must accept different correct implementations for each task while rejecting mutants, implementations with one required capability removed. The tasks span isolated mechanics, multi-system interactions, and repository-scale features. Across 20 combinations of language models and scaffolds, the best observed run solves 52.78% of tasks. Under Claude Code, all twelve models solve fewer tasks as task scope expands from isolated mechanics, through interacting systems, to repository-scale features. Agents inspect code more often and make more tool calls on repository-scale tasks than on isolated-mechanic tasks. Most unsuccessful submissions are runnable, but implement some required game behavior incorrectly. We compared versions of our benchmark evaluator built with and without validation using mutants. Without this validation, incorrect agent submissions passed. A separate analysis finds agents copying code from public repositories when network access is open. Reliable evaluation thus depends both on what the tests reject and on what external code agents can access.

</details>


### [24] [Labelling Bug-Fixing Commits with Local Open-Weight Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21616)
*Philip König,Georg Goldenits,Caroline König,Sebastian Raubitzek,Fabian Obermann,Dennis Toth,David Schmidt,Edgar Weippl,Kevin Mallinger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究能否仅凭提交内容（提交信息与代码差异）用本地开源权重语言模型识别缺陷修复提交，证明其显著优于关键词基线，并发布标注流水线与多语言银标准语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缺陷预测依赖缺陷修复提交标签，但各类标注途径（复用基准、缺陷跟踪链接、关键词匹配）均存在噪声与偏差：基准数据质量有记录问题、跟踪链接有偏且报告常被误分类、关键词启发式过于粗糙。因此需要一种不依赖问题跟踪系统、可复现、低成本、适用于私有代码的替代标注方法。

Method: 以人工验证与整理的真实缺陷修复数据集为基准，覆盖 Java、Python 和 JavaScript；将关键词基线与多个不同规模的开源权重模型进行比较，输入为提交信息与代码差异，模型在本地运行。同时分析评估语料有无负例对比较结果的影响。

Result: 在人工验证语料上，关键词基线只能召回不到一半的修复提交，而开源权重模型能召回绝大多数，并在逐个仓库比较中显著优于基线；模型规模更大并不稳定地带来更好效果。此外，不含负例的评估语料无法支撑考虑精确率的比较。

Conclusion: 仅凭提交内容即可用本地开源权重模型有效地将提交标注为缺陷修复，方法可复现、成本低、可用于私有代码且不依赖问题跟踪系统；作者发布标注流水线及推荐配置生成的多语言标注语料，作为可复现的银标准资源，用于构建当前的项目专属数据集。

Abstract: Defect prediction depends on knowing which commits fix bugs, yet the labels that encode this are produced by routes that each introduce noise. Reused benchmarks carry documented data-quality problems, issue-tracker links are biased and the underlying reports are frequently mistyped, and matching keywords in commit messages is a coarse heuristic. This paper examines whether commits can be labelled as bug fixes from their content alone, using open-weight language models that run locally and therefore keep the process reproducible, inexpensive at corpus scale, usable on proprietary code, and independent of any issue tracker. Against datasets of manually validated and curated bug fixes spanning Java, Python, and JavaScript, we compare a keyword baseline with a set of open-weight models of varying size, prompting each with the commit message and the code diff. On the manually validated corpus the keyword baseline recovers fewer than half of the fixes, whereas the open-weight models recover the large majority and outperform it repository by repository with statistical significance, and larger models do not consistently outperform smaller ones. We further show that evaluation corpora without negative examples cannot support a precision-aware comparison of such classifiers. We release the labelling pipeline together with a labelled, multi-language corpus produced by the recommended configuration, as a reproducible silver-standard resource for building current, project-specific datasets.

</details>


### [25] [From Smarter to Hungrier: the Role of Energy Efficiency in Software-defined Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21643)
*Ella Peltonen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 软件定义汽车在带来智能功能的同时增加了能耗，本文探讨其可持续性影响与环境伦理挑战，并强调需要新工具来测量、管理和优化能耗及可持续性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代汽车从机械实体转向软件定义设备，传感器复杂度、算力和网络能力的提升使更复杂的算法与应用成为可能；但车辆越智能，运行机器学习与人工智能功能所需能耗越大，由此引发对可持续性和环境伦理的担忧。作者希望厘清引入高资源消耗软硬件组件与对社会及可持续性影响之间的平衡。

Method: 本文为立场/讨论型研究，通过分析软件定义汽车发展趋势，讨论其能耗增长与可持续性、环境伦理之间的关系，并论证需要新型工具来测量、管理和优化能耗及其他可持续性指标，同时提出设计指导与可操作建议。

Result: 研究指出，日益增长的软件与硬件资源需求对可持续性构成威胁，并带来环境伦理问题；现有手段不足以应对，需要专门的工具与设计指南来量化和管理能耗及可持续性指标。

Conclusion: 软件定义汽车的发展必须在功能创新与可持续性之间取得平衡，应开发新工具并遵循设计指导与可操作建议，以测量、管理和优化能耗及其它可持续性指标。

Abstract: Automated features and advanced driving support systems have enabled a range of life-saving and comfort applications in modern personal vehicles. Development from mostly mechanical entities towards truly software-defined devices also means that added sensor complexity, processing power, and networking capabilities provide a platform for running even more complex algorithms and applications. As vehicles become smarter, they require even more energy to enable advanced machine learning- and artificial intelligence-based functionalities. This paper discusses the implications for sustainability and the threats to environmental ethics posed by the development of software-defined vehicles. We discuss the balance between introducing more resource-hungry software and hardware components and their effect on society and sustainability. We underscore the need for novel tools to measure, manage, and optimise energy consumption and other sustainability indicators, with design guidance and actionable recommendations.

</details>


### [26] [Supporting Industrial Test-Failure Analysis with LLM-Based Systems: An Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21843)
*Eric Jansson,Per Strandberg,Thomas Sörensen,Eduard Paul Enoiu,Wasif Afzal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文在工业环境中探索单智能体与多智能体编排的工具增强大模型系统在夜间测试失败根因分析（RCA）中的效果，六位从业者评估两个真实失败场景，并进行120次重复运行的运营测量。结果显示两种配置在感知质量上无一致优势，单智能体更快、成本更低，更适合作为实际基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夜间测试产生异构的测试数据与日志，从业者目前需要跨多个来源手动排查，成本高且费力。希望借助工具增强的大模型系统自动支持根因分析，并厘清单智能体与多智能体编排架构在真实工业场景中孰优孰劣。

Method: 采用探索性工业案例研究，在Westermo网络技术公司实现单智能体与编排式多智能体两种RCA工作流，均可访问测试元数据与日志；选取两个真实失败场景，由六位从业者通过问卷和焦点小组评估报告，并从120次重复执行中收集成本、时长、一致性等运营测量。

Result: 评估涵盖感知正确性、推理质量、修复现实性、清晰度、有用性与信任度。两种配置在两个场景中均未表现出一致的感知质量优势；单智能体系统生成报告更快且成本更低。

Conclusion: 在当前情境下，单智能体系统是更实用的基线方案；多智能体架构的潜在收益需要在更复杂的场景中进一步评估。

Abstract: This study examines tool-augmented Large Language Model (LLM) systems for supporting Root Cause Analysis (RCA) of nightly test failures at Westermo Network Technologies AB. Nightly test executions produce heterogeneous test data and logs that practitioners currently inspect manually across multiple sources. We implemented an RCA workflow in single-agent and orchestrated multi-agent configurations, both with access to test metadata and logs. An exploratory industrial case study used two real failure scenarios. Six practitioners evaluated the scenario reports through a survey and focus group, and operational measurements were collected from 120 repeated executions. The evaluation covered practitioner-perceived correctness, reasoning quality, fix realism, clarity, usefulness, and trust, as well as cost, duration, and consistency. Neither configuration showed a consistent practitioner-perceived quality advantage across the two scenarios. The single agent system generated reports faster and at lower cost, making it the more practical baseline in this context. The potential benefits of agent architectures require further evaluation in more complex scenarios.

</details>


### [27] [How Researchers Use and Verify AI Coding Assistants: Tasks and Validation Practices in Scientific Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22049)
*Gabrielle O'Brien,Reed Milewicz,Nasir Eisty*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基于2025年对科研编程者的527份自由文本调查，研究分析了研究者将哪些任务交给生成式AI以及如何评估其代码正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已进入科研编程，但关于研究者把哪些任务交给它、以及如何判断其代码是否正确，证据仍然很少。

Method: 对527份自由文本回答进行编码，识别任务类型与评估策略，并将其与编程经验、研究领域和信心评分相关联。

Result: 使用集中在五类任务：数据处理、可视化、调试、数学/科学计算和统计分析；评估方式非正式且个体化，超过一半提到运行生成代码，而自动化测试和他人评审罕见。使用场景与评估策略随编程经验变化不大，但信心有差异：经验较少者更信任AI而非自己，经验丰富者相反。评估信心与所报策略无关，最强相关因素是工具信心和自我信心。

Conclusion: 验证AI对科学代码的贡献主要依赖个人判断，缺乏共享的测试或评审基础设施；界面可支持与任务相匹配的评估，而非将其完全交给用户。

Abstract: Generative AI has entered research programming, yet there is little evidence about which tasks researchers hand to it or how they decide whether its code is correct. We draw on 527 free-text responses to a 2025 survey of researchers who write code, most of them at U.S. universities. In each response, a researcher recounts a single task from their own work, the way they used an AI tool for it, and what they did to assess the result. We coded the task and the evaluation strategies reported, and related both to programming experience, research area, and confidence ratings. Use was concentrated in five tasks: data handling, visualization, debugging, mathematical/scientific computing, and statistical analysis. Evaluation was informal and individual. Over half of accounts described running the generated code, while automated tests and review by another person were rare. Use cases and evaluation strategies varied little with programming experience, but confidence did: Less experienced programmers trusted the AI more than themselves, and experienced programmers the reverse. Evaluation confidence was not associated with the strategies reported. Its strongest correlates were confidence in the tool and in oneself. Validating AI contributions to scientific code rested largely on individual judgment, outside shared infrastructure for testing or review. Interfaces could support task-appropriate evaluation rather than leave it to the user.

</details>

### [28] [Sparse Priors for Efficient Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20883)
*Saumya Goyal,Barnabás Póczos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"稀疏先验"类与"稀疏维度"概念，证明基于k-稀疏先验的分布学习在下界为Ω(√(k/n))，并给出匹配上界，说明合适先验可克服关于样本量n的维度灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前从n个样本学习d维分布的理论保证以O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})退化，虽然被认为是极小极大最优，但作者假设这些界过于悲观，因为仅靠光滑性假设不足以刻画实际应用中分布的结构。

Method: 引入稀疏先验类，定义"稀疏维度"作为所有分布空间上先验稀疏性的度量；在k-稀疏先验下分析分布学习，推导贝叶斯风险下界，并在温和附加假设下给出TV距离的匹配上界；进一步证明贝叶斯设定下分布学习与学习采样的统计等价性。

Result: 在常见距离度量下，k-稀疏先验的分布学习达到Ω(√(k/n))的贝叶斯风险下界；对TV距离，在温和假设下得到匹配上界（至多相差对数项，在n、k上渐近）；结果同样适用于学习采样。

Conclusion: k仍可依赖维度d或内在维度，但结果表明：在合适的先验下，学习能够克服关于样本量n的维度灾难，说明现有界的悲观性源于对真实分布结构的忽视。

Abstract: Despite the widespread use and success of generative AI techniques today, theoretical guarantees on learning a distribution supported in $d$ dimensions from $n$ samples degrade as $O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})$, though shown to be minimax optimal. We hypothesize that present bounds are too pessimistic because smoothness assumptions are not enough to capture the structure of distributions that often appear in real applications. Consequently, we introduce the class of sparse priors and define the "Sparse Dimension" as a measure of sparsity of a prior over the space of all distributions. We show that distribution learning under a $k$-sparse prior achieves a Bayesian risk lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{k/n})$ under common distance metrics, and show a matching (up to logarithmic terms asymptotically in $n,k$) upper bound for the TV distance under mild additional assumptions. We show the statistical equivalence of distribution learning and learning to sample in the Bayesian setting so that our results apply to learning to sample as well. While $k$ can still depend on the dimension $d$, or a notion of intrinsic dimension, our results show that learning under an appropriate prior overcomes the curse of dimensionality with respect to the dependence on $n$.

</details>


### [29] [BI-Agent and BI-Bench: Towards Automating End-to-End Business Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20886)
*Chuxuan Hu,Yeye He,Penny Zhou,Wee Hyong Tok,Daniel Kang,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出首个端到端BI评测基准BI-Bench，发现前沿LLM准确率不足50%，并设计工具增强的BI-Agent及后训练框架，显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BI工作流需用户手动识别表、做数据转换、建立连接关系，才能回答业务问题，步骤复杂耗时，因此研究LLM能否端到端完成BI任务。

Method: 从公开来源收集真实BI项目，人工抽取(问题, 标准答案)对构建BI-Bench；设计将BI流程分解为搜索、连接、转换等子任务的工具增强BI-Agent；并开发后训练框架，从真实BI项目合成训练轨迹，进行SFT与RL训练。

Result: 前沿LLM在BI-Bench上准确率不足50%；BI-Agent相比原生LLM准确率提升最高40个百分点，后训练后BI-Agent再提升最高30个百分点。

Conclusion: 在复杂BI工作流中结合工具增强推理与领域专用后训练十分重要，为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) is a cornerstone of enterprise decision-making and is widely used by enterprise users in software such as Power BI and Tableau. In traditional BI workflows, users need to prepare data by (1) identifying relevant tables, (2) performing data transformations, and (3) building join relationships, before they can (4) answer their business questions. These steps can be complex and time-consuming, making BI challenging.
  Given the strong capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in working with data, we study their ability to answer BI questions end-to-end, without requiring users to manually perform the tedious preparation steps. To do this, we harvest a large collection of real-world BI projects from public sources, and manually extract pairs of (questions, ground-truth answers) from real user dashboards. The resulting benchmark, BI-Bench, is the first benchmark to systematically study LLMs' ability on end-to-end BI.
  We find that even frontier LLMs perform poorly on BI-Bench, with less than 50% accuracy. To address their limitations, we design a tool-augmented BI-Agent that decomposes BI workflows into subtasks on structured data, such as search, join, and transform, and orchestrates specialized data management methods across BI stages. Furthermore, we develop a post-training framework that synthesizes training trajectories from real BI projects, enabling BI-Agent to be further post-trained using both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). BI-Agent achieves substantial accuracy gains of up to 40 percentage points with vanilla LLMs, and post-trained BI-Agent yields gains of up to 30 points. Our results highlight the importance of combining tool-augmented reasoning with domain-specific post-training in complex BI workflows, and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [30] [Elastic Threshold Attention: Learned Contextual Sparsity for Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20888)
*Themistoklis Haris,Henry Li,Maryam Karimzadehgan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)，一种端到端可训练的动态稀疏注意力架构，通过从query表示预测上下文相关阈值实现硬件加速解码，同时保持稠密模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码中，海量KV缓存导致严重的内存带宽瓶颈。现有稀疏注意力方法虽能缓解，但依赖刚性启发式规则，会丢弃必要上下文，导致质量下降。作者希望在不牺牲稠密模型质量的前提下实现硬件加速解码。

Method: ETA从query表示直接预测动态、上下文化的阈值，为困难检索或推理步骤分配稠密式上下文，同时剪枝常规token。为避免表征坍缩，训练时对低于阈值的logits进行乘性抑制而非直接删除，形成平滑的均匀注意力底（分布式概率储备）。这消除了初始token上的局部注意力汇，并使推理时能够硬剪枝无信息KV块，吸收粗粒度GPU块选择误纳入的token。此外实现了Triton自定义解码核，用缓存的几何-概率边界在O(1)时间筛选KV块。并提出离线校准算法，冻结per-head常数阈值以消除预测器开销。

Result: 1.45B预训练ETA模型在语言建模、常识推理和长上下文针检索任务上媲美稠密注意力，训练稀疏度约85%，活跃解码密度约38%。推理时解码核在最长512K token序列上相对FlashAttention-2实现最高2.5倍的实际解码加速，离线校准额外减少27%注意力计算量。

Conclusion: ETA通过可学习的动态阈值注意力机制，在保持稠密模型质量的同时实现显著解码加速，兼顾训练稳定性与推理硬件效率，为长上下文稀疏注意力提供了端到端可训练的新范式。

Abstract: Massive KV caches can cause severe memory-bandwidth bottlenecks during long-context decoding. Sparse attention methods mitigate this via selective loading, but that comes at a cost: rigid heuristics drop necessary context, leading to quality degradation. We introduce \textbf{Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)}, an end-to-end trainable architecture that achieves hardware-accelerated decoding speed without sacrificing dense model quality. ETA predicts dynamic, contextual thresholds directly from query representations, allowing the model to allocate dense-like context to difficult retrieval or reasoning steps while pruning routine tokens. To learn this policy from scratch without representation collapse, ETA \emph{multiplicatively suppresses} sub-threshold logits toward zero during training rather than deleting them. Training against this smooth uniform attention floor provides a distributed probability reservoir that \textbf{causes localized attention sinks on initial tokens to disappear}. It also enables the model to hard-prune uninformative KV blocks at inference time and absorb incidental tokens co-admitted by coarse GPU block selection. As a result, a 1.45B pretrained ETA model rivals dense attention across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and long-context needle retrieval at $\approx 85\%$ training sparsity and $\approx 38\%$ active decode density. At inference time, we implement a custom decode kernel in Triton that screens KV blocks in $O(1)$ time using cached geometric-probabilistic bounds, delivering up to $2.5\times$ wall-clock decode speedups over FlashAttention-2 on sequences up to 512K tokens. Finally, we introduce an offline calibration algorithm for domain-specific deployments that freezes per-head constant thresholds to eliminate predictor overhead, cutting attention compute by an additional $27\%$.

</details>

*Boyuan Zhao,Sifan Zhang,Luping Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Bio-MF，一个无需预训练、无潜在空间的单步MeanFlow框架，用于由EEG条件生成fNIRS信号，实现快速且保留任务相关血流动力学结构的跨模态合成，在下游混合运动想象解码中提升准确率并大幅加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合MI-BCI结合EEG与fNIRS可优于纯EEG系统，但配对采集不便；现有EEG-to-fNIRS生成方法速度慢且常需预训练，难以满足实时场景。单步生成模型虽低延迟，却因去除迭代细化而降低保真度并引入非生理伪影。

Method: 提出Bio-MF：一种latent-free的单步MeanFlow框架。直接进行信号空间x预测，将其转化为MeanFlow速度监督，单次网络评估完成推理；并集成空间-时间交互4D编码、跨模态无分类器引导、噪声级门控FFT正则化，以在异构传感器布局下保留任务相关血流动力学结构。

Result: 在Dataset 1上，EEG+合成fNIRS相较纯EEG使ACC分别提升3.37（HbR）和4.15（HbO）个百分点；Dataset 2在未见64通道EEG蒙太奇下增益为2.98和2.50个百分点。在RTX PRO 6000 GPU上，生成一个fNIRS试次仅7.0 ms，相比1000步SCDM加速857倍。

Conclusion: Bio-MF能够在保持任务相关生成质量的同时实现快速EEG-to-fNIRS合成，支持下游混合MI解码。代码已开源。

Abstract: Hybrid motor-imagery brain-computer interfaces (MI-BCIs) combining EEG and fNIRS can outperform EEG-only systems by exploiting complementary electrophysiological and hemodynamic information. To obtain such hybrid information when paired EEG-fNIRS acquisition is unavailable or inconvenient, recent studies have focused on EEG-to-fNIRS cross-modal generation. However, existing methods still suffer from slow generation and often require pretraining, limiting their use in real-time MI-BCI scenarios. Although one-step generative models offer an attractive route to low-latency synthesis, removing the iterative refinement process can reduce generation fidelity and introduce non-physiological artifacts. To address these problems, this paper proposes Bio-MF, a latent-free one-step MeanFlow framework for EEG-conditioned fNIRS generation. Bio-MF performs direct signal-space x-prediction, converts this signal-space output into MeanFlow velocity supervision, and completes inference with one network evaluation. To preserve task-relevant hemodynamic structure under heterogeneous sensor layouts, Bio-MF integrates Spatial-Temporal Interactive 4D Encoding, cross-modal classifier-free guidance, and noise-level-gated FFT regularization. On Dataset 1, EEG + synthetic fNIRS improves ACC over EEG-only by 3.37 and 4.15 percentage points for HbR and HbO, respectively. On Dataset 2, the corresponding gains remain 2.98 and 2.50 percentage points under the unseen 64-channel EEG montage. On an RTX PRO 6000 GPU, Bio-MF generates one fNIRS trial in 7.0 ms, corresponding to an 857x speedup over the 1000-step SCDM latency. These results show that Bio-MF enables fast EEG-to-fNIRS synthesis while preserving task-relevant generation quality for downstream hybrid MI decoding. Our code is available at https://github.com/psychosiwa/Bio-MF.

</details>


### [32] [Continuous Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent and Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning from History of Astrophysical Time Series Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20906)
*Debartha Paul,Juncheng Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文回顾了用神经网络参数化的随机微分方程（SDE）拟合类星体光变曲线的方法，并提出了连续延迟记忆随机梯度下降（Continuous-Delayed-Memory SGD）及一种连续时间策略梯度的强化学习结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类星体亮度呈现随机变化，其光变曲线（时间序列）蕴含超大质量黑洞的信息；从地面巡天数据中对该随机变化进行建模是一个统计难题，需要借助 SDE 与神经网络参数化来刻画。

Method: 1）综述 SDE 结合神经网络参数化在历史上的应用；2）提出 Continuous-Delayed-Memory SGD，其更新依赖离散迭代过程的过去状态；3）在二维损失曲面上进行仿真，通过调整超参数对比 Vanilla SGD；4）提出一种带连续时间策略梯度的强化学习结构，用于探索性策略且无需求解 HJB 偏微分方程。

Result: 相比 Vanilla SGD，所提方法在二维曲面上表现出更广的探索范围和更精确的收敛行为；所提强化学习结构的最优性条件可退化为已有工作中的 Gibbs 策略。

Conclusion: 延迟记忆的连续随机梯度下降可改善探索与收敛表现，且连续时间策略梯度框架无需解 HJB 方程即可恢复 Gibbs 策略，为类星体光变等随机建模问题提供了理论与优化工具。

Abstract: Quasars are luminous objects in the universe that exhibit stochastic brightness variations encoding information about the supermassive black holes powering them, and modeling these variations from ground-based survey data time series, known as light curves, is a statistical challenge. This paper reviews how stochastic differential equations (SDEs) have been adapted with neural network parameterizations to overcome this challenge in history. We create the Continuous-Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent which depend on the past state of the discrete iteration process. We performed the simulation on some 2-dimensional landscape and observed some wider-exploration and more precise convergent behavior compared to Vanilla SGD by adjusting hyperparameters. Besides, we proposed a reinforcement learning structure with continuous time policy gradients for exploratory policies without solving HJB PDE, and we show that its optimality conditions recover the Gibbs policy of previous works.

</details>
